- **Cap (Guiv, 4ba8e1fb2 item 1, superseding 75cf9b82d item 3):** one pod, synthetic arm first, then bAbI; **hard stop min(5,400 s, 12:50Z)**. bAbI is NOT RUN if its own forecast fails.
